	if (ep17_2_main == 2) {
		cutin "bu_du5",2;
		mes "[???????????????]";
		mes "呜呜呜呜呜呜呜呜！！！！";
		mes "咕呃呃呃……！！！";
		next;
		cutin "",255;
		mes "- 我循着奇怪的声音看去，发现一个似乎快要溺水的人。-";
		next;
		if (select( "帮助他。", "不管他。" ) == 2) {
			cutin "bu_du5",2;
			mes "[???????????????]";
			mes "呜呜呜……";
			close2;
			cutin "",255;
			cloakonnpcself( "迪尤#ep172_bapw2-00" );
			end;
		}
		mes "- 他似乎已经无法行动。去给他找一片天地树叶吧。-";
		ep17_2_main = 3;
		erasequest 18001;
		setquest 11615;
		close2;
		cloakonnpcself( "迪尤#ep172_bapw2-00" );
		end;
	}
	if (ep17_2_main == 3) {
		if (countitem(610) < 1) {
		mes "- 他似乎已经无法行动。去给他找一片天地树叶吧。-";
			close;
		}
		cloakoffnpcself( "迪尤#ep172_bapw2-01" );
		mes "[迪尤]";
		mes "咳咳！！！！";
		mes "呜呜呜。";
		delitem 610,1;
		ep17_2_main = 4;
		erasequest 11615;
		setquest 11616;
		next;
		mes "[迪尤]";
		mes "呜哇！！！！";
		next;
		cutin "bu_du4",2;
		mes "[迪尤]";
		mes "咳嗯……";
		mes "我还以为自己要死了！";
		next;
		mes "[迪尤]";
		mes "谢谢你！！";
		mes "你救了我的命！";
		mes "我永远不会忘记你的恩情……";
		next;
		cutin "bu_du5",2;
		mes "[迪尤]";
		mes "……啊！！";
		mes "啊啊啊啊啊啊啊啊！！";
		next;
		cutin "bu_du2",2;
		mes "[迪尤]";
		mes "看看谁来了！！！";
		mes "" + strcharinfo(0) + "!!!";
		next;
		mes "[迪尤]";
		mes "能在这种地方再次遇到你，真是命运！！！";
		next;
		cutin "bu_du3",2;
		mes "[迪尤]";
		mes "如果不是你，我现在就会在普隆德拉教堂醒来！";
		mes "刚才我仿佛看到马克提着捐款袋，灿烂地对我微笑……";
		next;
		cutin "bu_du5",2;
		mes "[迪尤]";
		mes "太可怕了……我不想……再想起它……";
		next;
		cutin "bu_du4",2;
		mes "[迪尤]";
		mes "总之，先不说那个可怕的故事……你在这里救了我……";
		mes "你是来调查宅邸的吗？";
		next;
		cutin "bu_du2",2;
		mes "[迪尤]";
		mes "不愧是我认可的对手！";
		mes "这里不是很棒吗？？？";
		mes "真不敢相信这个地方一直隐藏到现在……";
		next;
	}
	if (ep17_2_main == 4) {
		cloakoffnpcself( "迪尤#ep172_bapw2-01" );
		cutin "bu_du1",2;
		mes "[迪尤]";
		mes "现在不是说这些的时候！";
		mes "我们上去！！！";
		mes "现在！！";
		next;
		mes "[迪尤]";
		mes "我敢肯定其他人会把所有乐趣都抢走！！！";
		next;
		cutin "bu_du3",2;
		mes "[迪尤]";
		mes "好了，我们去和马克他们汇合吧。";
		mes "嗯……";
		mes "哦哦哦哦哦哦哦哦！！";
		next;
		cutin "bu_du1",2;
		mes "[迪尤]";
		mes "好了，在那边！";
		mes "我好像听到玛姬的声音了！！！";
		close2;
		cutin "",255;
		navigateto("ba_pw01",14,115);
		cloakonnpcself( "迪尤#ep172_bapw2-01" );
		end;
	}
	mes "- 这似乎是净化污水的装置。-";
	close;

OnInit:
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "isbegin_quest(18001) == 1 && BaseLevel > 130" );
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "isbegin_quest(11615) == 1 && countitem(610) > 0" );
	end;
}

ba_pw01,87,290,3	script	阿尔普#ep172_bapw1-00	4_M_BLACKMAN,{
	if (ep17_2_main < 5) {
		cutin "bu_alp3",2;
		mes "[阿尔普]";
		mes "这台装置可以为一座城市供能……不，它能提供国家规模的能源。";
		mes "这台装置究竟是做什么的？";
		close3;
	}
	if (ep17_2_main == 5 || ep17_2_main == 6) {
		if (ep17_2_main == 5) {
			ep17_2_main = 6;
			erasequest 11617;
			setquest 11618;
		}
		cutin "bu_alp3",2;
		mes "[阿尔普]";
		mes "你来了……";
		mes "这边……";
		close2;
		cutin "",255;
		navigateto("ba_in01",20,319);
		warp "ba_in01",23,315;
		end;
	}
	mes "[阿尔普]";
	mes "这些装置真惊人……";
	mes "它们能为一座城市……不，能为整个国家供能。";
	mes "究竟是为了什么，才做到这种程度……";
	cutin "bu_alp1.bmp",2;
	close3;

OnInit:
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "isbegin_quest(11617) == 1" );
	end;
}

ba_maison,70,145,3	script	塔玛琳#ep172_bamaison-00	4_EP17_TAMARIN,{
	if (checkweight(1000103,5) == 0) {
		mes "- 你持有的物品太多。";
		mes "请整理物品栏后再试。-";
		close;
	}
	if (ep17_2_main == 7 || ep17_2_main == 8) {
		if (ep17_2_main == 7) {
			cutin "ep172_tamarin02",2;

			cloakoffnpcself( "迪尤#ep172_bamaison-00" );

		mes "他们说要登记才能在宅邸里活动，所以我登记后，他们突然把我的衣服换成了这样！";

		mes "别笑了！";

	}
	end;

	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "isbegin_quest(11620) == 1" );

	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST2, QMARK_YELLOW, "checkquest(11638,HUNTING) == 2" );
	end;
}

			}
			cutin "ep162_est01",2;
			mes "[恩斯特]";
			mes "再等一会儿再出去吧……不能大意。";
			close3;

		cloakoffnpcself( "玛真塔#172ba01" );
		cloakoffnpcself( "青岚#172ba01" );
		end;
	}
	end;

OnInit:
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "ep17_2_main >= 9 && ep17_2_bath == 0" );
	end;
}

		mes "谢谢。那么你能消灭^0000CD两种故障的搓澡师各 10 个^000000吗？我还需要 5 个^0000CD损坏自动人偶的零件^000000。";
		next;
		cutin "",255;
		select( "当然。" );
		cutin "ep172_beta",2;
		mes "[锅炉]";
		mes "好，那就拜托了。";
		setquest 17375;
		completequest 17375;
		setquest 17376;
		close3;
	}
	cutin "ep172_beta",2;
	mes "[锅炉]";
	mes "今天怎么来这里了？哦，是来工作的吗？";
	next;
	.@playtime = checkquest(17377,PLAYTIME);
	.@hunting = checkquest(17376,HUNTING);

	if (.@playtime == 0 || .@playtime == 1)
		.@menu$ = "^C0C0C0帮忙工作 - 冷却中^000000";
	else if (.@hunting == 0 || .@hunting == 1)
		.@menu$ = "^C0C0C0帮忙工作 - 进行中^000000";
	else
		.@menu$ = "帮忙工作";

	if (select( .@menu$, "我来打个招呼。" ) == 2) {
		mes "[锅炉]";
		mes "是啊，我不知道你为什么来。这里没有变化，我们还在处理锅炉。";
		close3;
	}
	if (.@playtime == 0 || .@playtime == 1) {
		mes "[锅炉]";
		mes "嗯……我听说人类工作过度很容易坏掉。请先休息，距离上次交付满 4 小时后再回来。";
		close3;
	}
	switch( .@hunting ) {
	case -1:
		if (select( "交给我吧。", "我今天想休息一下。" ) == 2) {
			mes "[锅炉]";
			mes "真奇怪。我以为你来找我就是想工作，对吧？好吧，我明白了。";
			close3;
		}
		mes "[锅炉]";
		mes "怎么会有这么真诚的人类？那么今天也请消灭^0000CD两种故障的搓澡师各 10 个^000000，并带来 5 个^0000CD损坏自动人偶的零件^000000。";
		setquest 17376;
		if (isbegin_quest(17377) == 1)
			erasequest 17377;
		close3;
	case 0:
	case 1:
		mes "[锅炉]";
		mes "工作完成了吗？";
		next;
		mes "[锅炉]";
		if (countitem(1000231) < 5) {
			mes "在哪里？我们需要 5 个损坏自动人偶的零件。我觉得你的背包有洞，应该检查一下。";
			close3;
		}
		mes "你消灭了两种故障的搓澡师各 10 个吗？你哪里受伤了吗？";
		close3;
	case 2:
		if (countitem(1000231) < 5) {
			mes "[锅炉]";
			mes "在哪里？我们需要 5 个损坏自动人偶的零件。我觉得你的背包有洞，应该检查一下。";
			close3;
		}
		mes "[锅炉]";
		mes "工作完成了吗？";
		next;
		mes "[锅炉]";
		mes "你带来了很多。好吧，我用这张巴米尔券交换。谢谢。";
		erasequest 17376;
		setquest 17377;
		delitem 1000231,5;
		getitem rand(100147,100149),1;
		getitem 1000103,2;
		getexp 834974,584500;
		close3;
	}
	end;

OnInit:
	// Sidequest quest : Exploring bathhouse
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "isbegin_quest(17364) == 1" );

	// Daily
	questinfo( QTYPE_DAILYQUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "isbegin_quest(17374) == 2 && checkquest(17376,HUNTING) == -1 && checkquest(17377,PLAYTIME) == -1" );
	questinfo( QTYPE_DAILYQUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "checkquest(17377,PLAYTIME) == 2" );
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST2, QMARK_YELLOW, "checkquest(17376,HUNTING) == 2 && countitem(1000231) >= 5" );
	end;

		setquest 17380;

		getexp 722940,506070;
		close3;

	end;

	// Daily quests
	cutin "ep172_beta",2;
	mes "[奇普]";
	mes "你是来履行我们的约定的吗？";
	mes "你想接下哪项工作？";
	next;
	.@quest_menu$[0] = callsub( S_Menu, 17359,17360, "仓库清理" );
	.@quest_menu$[1] = callsub( S_Menu, 17361,17362, "地下室清理" );

	switch( select( .@quest_menu$[0], .@quest_menu$[1], "有什么问题吗？" ) ) {
	case 1:
		.@lvl = 160;
		.@hunt = 17359;
		.@cooldown = 17360;

		.@cooldown = 17362;
		break;
	case 3:
		cutin "ep172_beta",2;
		mes "[奇普]";
		mes "目前还没有出问题，但没人知道能持续多久。不要放松警惕。";
		close3;
	}

	case -1:
		break;
	case 0:
	case 1:
		mes "[奇普]";
		mes "如果我们制造太大声音，地下室里的东西可能会来到这里。请在距离上次交付满4小时后再回来。";
		close3;
	case 2:
		erasequest .@cooldown;
		break;
	}
	switch( checkquest(.@hunt,HUNTING) ) {
	case -1:
		if (.@hunt == 17359) {
			mes "[奇普]";
			mes "你确实喜欢战斗。那么请处理仓库里的 ^0000CD20 个邪心猎人侦察兵^000000。";

		mes "[奇普]";

			mes "[奇普]";
			mes "你没看到它们到处乱跑吗？";
			mes "那么请处理仓库里的 ^0000CD20 个邪心猎人侦察兵^000000。";
			close3;
		}
		mes "[奇普]";
		mes "你没看到它们到处乱跑吗？";
		mes "那么请处理地下室里的 ^0000CD20 个故障的仓库管理员^000000。";
		close3;
	case 2:
		mes "[奇普]";
		mes "你已经处理完所有事情了吗？";
		next;
		mes "[奇普]";
		mes "我可以确认数量已经减少了。";
		mes "现在我可以开始工作了。";
		mes "谢谢。请在距离上次交付满4小时后再回来。";

		erasequest .@hunt;
		getitem 1000103,1;
		if (.@hunt == 17359)
			getexp 4431108,3101780;
		else
			getexp 5974540,4182156;
		close3;
	}
	end;

S_Menu:
	.@string$ = getarg(2);

	switch( checkquest(getarg(1),PLAYTIME) ) {
	case -1:
		switch( checkquest(getarg(0),HUNTING) ) {
		case -1:
			.@menu$ = .@string$;
			break;
		case 0:
		case 1:
			.@menu$ = "^777777" + .@string$ + " - 进行中^000000";
			break;
		case 2:
			.@menu$ = "" + .@string$ + " - 已完成";
			break;
		}
		break;
	case 0:
	case 1:
		.@menu$ = "^777777" + .@string$ + " - 等待中^000000";
		break;
	case 2:
		.@menu$ = .@string$;
		break;
	}
	return .@menu$;

OnInit:
	// sidequest
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "BaseLevel >= 160 && ep17_2_main >= 9 && ep17_2_chirp == 0" );
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST2, QMARK_YELLOW, "checkquest(17342,HUNTING) == 2" );
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST2, QMARK_YELLOW, "isbegin_quest(17344) == 1" );
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST2, QMARK_YELLOW, "isbegin_quest(17358) == 1" );

	// daily
	questinfo( QTYPE_DAILYQUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "isbegin_quest(17358) == 2 && checkquest(17359,HUNTING) == -1 && checkquest(17360,PLAYTIME) == -1" );
	questinfo( QTYPE_DAILYQUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "checkquest(17360,PLAYTIME) == 2" );
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST2, QMARK_YELLOW, "checkquest(17359,HUNTING) == 2" );

	questinfo( QTYPE_DAILYQUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "isbegin_quest(17358) == 2 && BaseLevel >= 170 && checkquest(17361,HUNTING) == -1 && checkquest(17362,PLAYTIME) == -1" );
	questinfo( QTYPE_DAILYQUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "checkquest(17362,PLAYTIME) == 2" );
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST2, QMARK_YELLOW, "checkquest(17361,HUNTING) == 2" );
	end;
}

S_Menu:
	.@string$ = getarg(4);

	switch( checkquest(getarg(3),PLAYTIME) ) {
	case -1:
		for ( .@i = 0; .@i < 3; ++.@i ) {
			if (checkquest(getarg(.@i),HUNTING) == 0 || checkquest(getarg(.@i),HUNTING) == 1)
				return "^777777[进行中]^000000 " + .@string$;
		}
		.@menu$ = "[每日] " + .@string$;
		break;
	case 0:
	case 1:
		.@menu$ = "^777777[等待中]^000000 " + .@string$;
		break;
	case 2:
		.@menu$ = "[每日] " + .@string$;
		break;
	}
	return .@menu$;

OnMove:
	.@gid = getnpcid(0);
	npcspeed rand(50,200);
	do {
		.@x = rand(16,31);
		.@y = rand(311,332);
	}
	while (!checkcell("ba_in01",.@x,.@y,cell_chkpass));

	unitstopwalk .@gid, USW_FORCE_STOP;
	unitwalk .@gid,.@x,.@y;
	switch( rand(1,11) ) {
	case 1:
		npctalk "(◀▶ω◀▶)";
		break;
	case 2:
		npctalk "☜(^▽^)☞Ξ≡=З";
		break;
	case 3:
		npctalk "(≥▽≤)♥♡♥♡♥♡";
		break;
	case 4:
		npctalk "(⊙ω⊙)";
		break;
	case 5:
		npctalk "(●㉦●)";
		break;
	case 6:
		npctalk "(○ω○)";
		break;
	case 7:
		npctalk "(♡З♡)~♥";
		break;
	case 8:
		npctalk "(◎ω◎)";
		break;
	case 9:
		npctalk "(*'▼'*)b";
		break;
	default:
		break;
	}
	sleep rand(1500,5000);
	donpcevent strnpcinfo(0) + "::OnMove";
	end;

OnInit:
	// (commented, no questinfo)
	// questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "ep17_2_main >= 9 && isbegin_quest(5892) == 0" );

	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST2, QMARK_YELLOW, "checkquest(5893,HUNTING) == 2 && checkquest(5894,HUNTING) == 2 && checkquest(5895,HUNTING) == 2" );
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST2, QMARK_YELLOW, "checkquest(5897,HUNTING) == 2 && checkquest(5898,HUNTING) == 2 && checkquest(5899,HUNTING) == 2" );

	questinfo( QTYPE_DAILYQUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "isbegin_quest(5892) == 2 && checkquest(5896,PLAYTIME) == -1 && checkquest(5893,HUNTING) == -1" );
	questinfo( QTYPE_DAILYQUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "isbegin_quest(5892) == 2 && checkquest(5900,PLAYTIME) == -1 && checkquest(5897,HUNTING) == -1" );

	questinfo( QTYPE_DAILYQUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "isbegin_quest(5892) == 2 && checkquest(5896,PLAYTIME) == 2" );
	questinfo( QTYPE_DAILYQUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "isbegin_quest(5892) == 2 && checkquest(5900,PLAYTIME) == 2" );
	donpcevent strnpcinfo(0) + "::OnMove";
	end;
}

	}
	if (ep17_2_library == 4) {
		if (F_queststatus(HAVEQUEST, 11629,11630,11631,11632,11633) != 1) {
			cutin "ep172_beta",2;
			mes "[宅邸管家·贝塔]";

	cutin "ep172_beta",2;
	mes "[宅邸管家·贝塔]";
	mes "您好，" + strcharinfo(0) + "。";
	mes "以下委托仅面向图书馆用户，期待得到您的协助。";
	next;
	.@quest_menu$[0] = callsub( S_Menu, 11634,11635, "终极书虫" );
	.@quest_menu$[1] = callsub( S_Menu, 11636,11637, "终极魔法书" );

	switch( select( .@quest_menu$[0], .@quest_menu$[1], "取消" ) ) {
	case 1:
		.@hunt = 11634;
		.@cooldown = 11635;
		setarray .@exp[0],910040,637040;
		break;
	case 2:
		.@hunt = 11636;
		.@cooldown = 11637;
		setarray .@exp[0],934000,653800;
		break;
	case 3:
		mes "[宅邸管家·贝塔]";
		mes "祝您平安，度过舒适的时光。";
		close3;
	}
	switch( checkquest(.@cooldown,PLAYTIME) ) {
	case -1:
		break;
	case 0:
	case 1:
		mes "[宅邸管家·贝塔]";
		mes "今天的工作已经完成了。";
		mes "谢谢。";
		close3;
	case 2:
		erasequest .@cooldown;
		break;
	}
	switch( checkquest(.@hunt,HUNTING) ) {
	case -1:
		if (.@hunt == 11634) {
			mes "[宅邸管家·贝塔]";
			mes "最近有人反映，以魔法书为食的书虫数量激增，给图书馆的使用造成了不便。";
			mes "请处理 20 只书虫，改善图书馆环境。";
		}
		else {
			mes "[宅邸管家·贝塔]";
			mes "最近有人反映，流浪魔法书离开指定区域，导致图书馆书架一团糟。";
			mes "请消灭附近的 20 只流浪魔法书，改善图书馆环境。";
		}
		next;
		if (select( "接受", "拒绝" ) == 2) {
			mes "[宅邸管家·贝塔]";
			mes "希望你平安，旅途愉快。";
			close3;
		}
		mes "[宅邸管家·贝塔]";
		mes "非常感谢。";
		setquest .@hunt;
		close3;
	case 0:
	case 1:
		mes "[宅邸管家·贝塔]";
		mes "这个任务已经在进行中。";
		close3;
	case 2:
		mes "[宅邸管家·贝塔]";
		mes "我已经确认委托完成。";
		mes "谢谢。";
		setquest .@cooldown;
		erasequest .@hunt;
		getitem 1000103,2;
		getexp .@exp[0],.@exp[1];
		close3;
	}
	end;

S_Menu:
	.@string$ = getarg(2);

	switch( checkquest(getarg(1),PLAYTIME) ) {
	case -1:
		switch( checkquest(getarg(0),HUNTING) ) {
		case -1:
			.@menu$ = .@string$ + " [130]";
			break;
		case 0:
		case 1:
			.@menu$ = "^777777" + .@string$ + "（进行中）^000000";
			break;
		case 2:
			.@menu$ = .@string$ + " [130]";
			break;
		}
		break;
	case 0:
	case 1:
		.@menu$ = "^777777" + .@string$ + "（等待中）^000000";
		break;
	case 2:
		.@menu$ = .@string$ + " [130]";
		break;
	}
	return .@menu$;

OnInit:
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "isbegin_quest(11623) == 1" );
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "isbegin_quest(11629) == 1 && isbegin_quest(11630) == 1 && isbegin_quest(11631) == 1 && isbegin_quest(11632) == 1 && isbegin_quest(11633) == 1" );

	// Daily quests
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST2, QMARK_YELLOW, "checkquest(11634,HUNTING) == 2" );
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST2, QMARK_YELLOW, "checkquest(11636,HUNTING) == 2" );

	questinfo( QTYPE_DAILYQUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "checkquest(11635,PLAYTIME) == 2" );
	questinfo( QTYPE_DAILYQUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "checkquest(11637,PLAYTIME) == 2" );

	questinfo( QTYPE_DAILYQUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "ep17_2_library == 5 && checkquest(11634,HUNTING) == -1 && checkquest(11635,PLAYTIME) == -1" );
	questinfo( QTYPE_DAILYQUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "ep17_2_library == 5 && checkquest(11636,HUNTING) == -1 && checkquest(11637,PLAYTIME) == -1" );
	end;
}

		mes "- 携带的物品太多，无法继续任务。请清理背包后再试。-";
		close;
	}
	if (BaseLevel < 150) {
		mes "[棉花]";
		mes "嗯，你看起来太弱，无法工作。我觉得你现在做不到。";
		next;
		mes "- 至少达到150级才能进行此任务。-";
		close;
	}

		mes "- 我与养殖区前的自动人偶对上了视线。-";
		next;
		mes "- 人偶上下打量我一番，然后转过头去。-";
		close;
	}
	switch( checkquest(8595,PLAYTIME) ) {
	case -1:
		break;
	case 0:
	case 1:
		mes "[棉花]";
		mes "你今天已经吃够草了……距离上次交付满4小时后再来吧，亲切的客人！";
		close;
	case 2:
		mes "[棉花]";
		mes "看来你休息够了，要再和食人火龙果玩吗？";
		erasequest 8595;
		close;
	}
	setarray .@quest_id[0],8590,8591,8592,8593,8594;

	for ( .@i = 0; .@i < 5; ++.@i ) {
		switch( checkquest(.@quest_id[.@i],HUNTING) ) {
		case -1:
			break;
		case 0:
		case 1:
			switch( .@quest_id[.@i] ) {
				case 8590: .@color$ = "^FF45FF红肉"; break;
				case 8591: .@color$ = "^FFBF8C黄肉"; break;
				case 8592: .@color$ = "^0AFFC8蓝肉"; break;
				case 8593: .@color$ = "^A679BF紫肉"; break;
				case 8594: .@color$ = "^9ABF94绿肉"; break;
			}
			mes "[棉花]";
			mes "请在农场照顾 20 只" + .@color$ + "食人火龙果^000000……不，我是说抚摸它们！";
			close;
		case 2:
			switch( .@quest_id[.@i] ) {
				case 8590: .@color$ = "红色的痕迹"; break;
				case 8591: .@color$ = "黄色的痕迹"; break;
				case 8592: .@color$ = "蓝色的痕迹"; break;
				case 8593: .@color$ = "紫色的痕迹"; break;
				case 8594: .@color$ = "绿色的痕迹"; break;
			}
			mes "[棉花]";
			mes "啊！你身上有很多" + .@color$ + "……啊，看来你和食人火龙果玩过了。";
			mes "谢谢！";
			mes "这是你辛苦工作的报酬。";
			setquest 8595;
			erasequest .@quest_id[.@i];
			getitem 1000103,2;
			getexp 1800000,1320000;
			next;
			mes "[棉花]";
			mes "4小时后也请再来帮忙！";
			close;
		}
	}
	mes "[棉花]";
	mes "你是来帮助我们的吗？如果不介意，可以和农场里的食人火龙果玩吗？当然会给你奖励。";
	next;
	switch( select( "接受委托。", "拒绝委托。", "询问详情。" ) ) {
	case 1:
		.@quest = .@quest_id[rand(5)];
		switch( .@quest ) {
			case 8590: .@color$ = "^FF45FF红肉"; break;
			case 8591: .@color$ = "^FFBF8C黄肉"; break;
			case 8592: .@color$ = "^0AFFC8蓝肉"; break;

	case 1:

		erasequest 3391;

	}

			mes "由于最近发生了入侵事件，人偶数量大幅减少，我们的园丁现在无法应付这种棘手的植物。";
			next;
			mes "[西休]";
			mes "你能进入水之花园，猎捕并为我带回一株^0000CD格兰帕皮利亚^000000吗？";
			close3;
		case 2:
			cutin "ep172_beta",2;
			mes "[西休]";
			mes "你已经带回了一株格兰帕皮利亚。";
			mes "我已经向里面的一名园丁确认过了。";
			next;
			mes "[西休]";
			mes "谢谢你帮助园丁们。";
			mes "下次如果你有时间，我还会再请你帮忙。";
			next;
			mes "[西休]";
			mes "我会给你一些巴米尔券作为谢礼。你可以在宅邸餐厅自由使用。";
			close2;
			cutin "",255;
			setquest 16434;
			erasequest 16433;
			getitem 1000103,4;
			getexp 8000000,5000000;
			end;
		}
		break;
	case 3:
		switch( checkquest(16436,PLAYTIME) ) {
		case -1:
			break;
		case 0:
		case 1:
			cutin "ep172_beta",2;
			mes "[西休]";
			mes "你需要休息一下。哦，不只是你，水之花园也需要休息。";
			mes "园丁们安排了植物培养，所以今天到此为止。";
			next;
			mes "[西休]";
			mes "请先休息，距离上次交付满4小时后再回来。";
			mes "我很乐意再次请求你的帮助。";
			close3;
		case 2:
			erasequest 16436;
			break;
		}
		if (isbegin_quest(16435) == 1) {
			if (countitem(1000099) > 0) {
				cutin "ep172_beta",2;
				mes "[西休]";
				mes "检查过了吗？园丁们怎么样？";
				mes "我收到消息，人偶们很安全。我猜它只是忘了定期联系。";
				next;
				mes "[西休]";
				mes "园丁总是忘记联系我，所以作为这片区域的管理者，我得进行全面检查。";
				mes "也许入侵者正在制造某种骚动。总之，看来唯一的办法是每次检查人数。";
				next;
				cutin "",255;
				select( "交出你收到的饼干。" );
				cutin "ep172_beta",2;
				mes "[西休]";
				mes "……饼干？";
				mes "你收到了这个？当然，这是只有园丁才有的零食，也可以用来确认他们平安。";
				next;
				mes "[西休]";
				mes "总之，谢谢你检查。拿些饼干吧。据说味道很好。这种饼干能滋养花园里的动植物，客人也很喜欢。";
				next;
				mes "[西休]";
				mes "我会给你一些巴米尔券作为帮助的回报。你可以在宅邸餐厅自由使用。";
				close2;
				cutin "",255;
				setquest 16436;
				erasequest 16435;
				delitem 1000099,countitem(1000099);
				getitem 1000103,4;
				getexp 800000,500000;
				end;
			}
			cutin "ep172_beta",2;
			mes "[西休]";
			mes "园丁们通常在迷宫花园失去联系。";
			mes "请进入水之花园，确认园丁是否安好。";
			close3;
		}
		cutin "ep172_beta",2;
		mes "[西休]";
		mes "由于最近的入侵者事件和植物变化，园丁经常更换。不过，有些进入水之花园的园丁失去了联系。";
		next;
		mes "[西休]";
		mes "我猜花园出了问题，因为和另一位园丁的联系经常中断。";
		next;
		mes "[西休]";
		mes "你能进去检查是网络问题、通信失败，还是确认其他园丁是否安好吗？";
		next;
		cutin "",255;
		if (select( "我去替你检查。", "拒绝。" ) == 2)
			break;
		cutin "ep172_beta",2;
		mes "[西休]";
		mes "谢谢。如果遇到园丁，请告诉他们联系我。";
		close2;
		cutin "",255;
		setquest 16435;
		end;
	case 4:
		break;
	}
	cutin "ep172_beta",2;
	mes "[西休]";
	mes "你有时间吗？有人帮忙总是好的。我会在这里。如果你有时间，请来和我谈谈。";
	close3;

OnInit:
	// Story
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "isbegin_quest(16428) == 1" );
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "checkquest(16429,HUNTING) == 2" );

	// Daily quests
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST2, QMARK_YELLOW, "checkquest(16431,HUNTING) == 2" );
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST2, QMARK_YELLOW, "checkquest(16433,HUNTING) == 2" );
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST2, QMARK_YELLOW, "isbegin_quest(16435) == 1 && countitem(1000099) > 1" );

	questinfo( QTYPE_DAILYQUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "ep172_watergarden == 5 && checkquest(16432,PLAYTIME) == -1 && checkquest(16431,HUNTING) == -1" );
	questinfo( QTYPE_DAILYQUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "ep172_watergarden == 5 && BaseLevel >= 180 && checkquest(16434,PLAYTIME) == -1 && checkquest(16433,HUNTING) == -1" );
	questinfo( QTYPE_DAILYQUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "ep172_watergarden == 5 && checkquest(16436,PLAYTIME) == -1 && isbegin_quest(16435) == 0" );

	questinfo( QTYPE_DAILYQUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "checkquest(16432,PLAYTIME) == 2" );
	questinfo( QTYPE_DAILYQUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "checkquest(16434,PLAYTIME) == 2" );
	questinfo( QTYPE_DAILYQUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "checkquest(16436,PLAYTIME) == 2" );
	end;
}
